          // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays view of the option set, used for coalesced device access.\n// The pricing inputs live in pinned host memory so that host/device transfers can\n// overlap with kernel execution.\nstruct OptionSoA {\n    double* strike = nullptr;\n    double* spot = nullptr;\n    double* q = nullptr;\n    double* r = nullptr;\n    double* t = nullptr;\n    double* vol = nullptr;\n    uint8_t* type = nullptr;\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options, one option per thread.\n//\n// Both the call and the put expression are evaluated unconditionally and selected at\n// the end: within a warp the option types are mixed, so a branch would serialize the\n// two paths anyway. N(-d) is formed as 0.5 * (1 - erf(d / sqrt(2))), which is the same\n// sequence of floating-point operations the scalar code performs for\n// cumulativeNormal(-d) because erf is exactly odd.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ strikeArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ spotArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ qArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ rArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ tArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ volArr,\n                                                          const uint8_t* __restrict__ typeArr,\n                                                          double* __restrict__ prices,\n                                                          const size_t n) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double S = spotArr[i];\n    const   prices[i] = 0.0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double e1 = erf(d1 * M_SQRT1_2);\n    const double e2 = erf(d2 * M_SQRT1_2);\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double spotDiscount = S * exp(-q * T);\n\n    const double call = spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e1)) - K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e2));\n    const double put = K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e2)) - spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e1));\n\n    prices[i] = (typeArr[i] == CALL) ? call : put;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, OptionSoA& soa, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n\n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n\n        soa.strike[i] = options[i].strike;\n        soa.spot[i] = options[i].spot;\n        soa.q[i] = options[i].q;\n        soa.r[i] = options[i].r;\n        soa.t[i] = options[i].t;\n        soa.vol[i] = options[i].vol;\n        soa.type[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(options[i].type);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: option.t;\n    const double sigma = option.vol;\n    \n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        return 0.0;\n    }\n    \n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n    \n    const double Nd1 = cumulativeNormal(d1);\n    const double Nd2 = cumulativeNormal(d2);\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    \n    double price;\n    if (option.type == CALL) {\n        price = S * exp(-q * T) * Nd1 - K * discount * Nd2;\n    } else { // PUT\n        price = K * discount * cumulativeNormal(-d2) - S * exp(-q * T) * cumulativeNormal(-d1);\n    }\n    \n    return price;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n    \n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n        \n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options, \n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n    \n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n        \n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\", \n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n        \n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions =           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays view of the option set, used for coalesced device access.\n// The pricing inputs live in pinned host memory so that host/device transfers can\n// overlap with kernel execution.\nstruct OptionSoA {\n    double* strike = nullptr;\n    double* spot = nullptr;\n    double* q = nullptr;\n    double* r = nullptr;\n    double* t = nullptr;\n    double* vol = nullptr;\n    uint8_t* type = nullptr;\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options, one option per thread.\n//\n// Both the call and the put expression are evaluated unconditionally and selected at\n// the end: within a warp the option types are mixed, so a branch would serialize the\n// two paths anyway. N(-d) is formed as 0.5 * (1 - erf(d / sqrt(2))), which is the same\n// sequence of floating-point operations the scalar code performs for\n// cumulativeNormal(-d) because erf is exactly odd.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ strikeArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ spotArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ qArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ rArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ tArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ volArr,\n                                                          const uint8_t* __restrict__ typeArr,\n                                                          double* __restrict__ prices,\n                                                          const size_t n) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double S = spotArr[i];\n    const double K = strikeArr[i];\n    const double r = rArr[i];\n    const double q = qArr[i];\n    const double T = tArr[i];\n    const double sigma = volArr[i];\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        prices[i] = 0.0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double e1 = erf(d1 * M_SQRT1_2);\n    const double e2 = erf(d2 * M_SQRT1_2);\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double spotDiscount = S * exp(-q * T);\n\n    const double call = spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e1)) - K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e2));\n    const double put = K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e2)) - spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e1));\n\n    prices[i] = (typeArr[i] == CALL) ? call : put;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 0.35, 5.0975, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0205, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {CALL, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9413, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 90.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 9.9210, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 100.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 1.8734, 1.0e-3},\n        {PUT, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, OptionSoA& soa, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n\n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n\n        soa.strike[i] = options[i].strike;\n        soa.spot[i] = options[i].spot;\n        soa.q[i] = options[i].q;\n        soa.r[i] = options[i].r;\n        soa.t[i] = options[i].t;\n        soa.vol[i] = options[i].vol;\n        soa.type[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(options[i].type);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Black-Scholes Option Pricing Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of options: %zu\\n\", numOptions);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    if (numOptions == 0) {\n        printf(\"Pricing options...\\n\");\n        printf(\"Computation time: %.3f ms\\n\", 0.0);\n        std::vector<double> empty;\n        if (printResults) {\n            print_results(empty, \"OptionPrices\");\n        }\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating results...\\n\");\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n        }\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    // Initialize the device up front so that context creation is not part of the\n    // measured region.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));\n\n    // Pinned staging buffers for the inputs and the prices.\n    OptionSoA soa;\n    double* h_prices = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.strike, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.spot, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.q, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.r, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.t, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.vol, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.type, numOptions * sizeof(uint8_t), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_prices, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n\n    // Generate options\n    std::vector<OptionInput> options;\n    generateOptions(options, soa, numOptions);\n\n    // The work is split into chunks that are streamed through the device so that the\n    // host-to-device transfer of one chunk overlaps the kernel and the device-to-host\n    // transfer of its predecessors.\n    constexpr int NUM_STREAMS = 4;\n    constexpr size_t MAX_CHUNK = 1ull << 20;\n    constexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n    const size_t chunkSize = std::min(numOptions, MAX_CHUNK);\n    const size_t numChunks = (numOptions + chunkSize - sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in[s].t, chunkSize * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in[s].vol, chunkSize * sizeof(double)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_in[s].type, chunkSize * sizeof(uint8_t)));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d_prices[s], chunkSize * sizeof(double)));\n    }\n\n    // Price options\n    printf(\"Pricing CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_in[s].spot, soa.spot + offset, count * sizeof(double),\n                                   cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, stream));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyAsync(d_in[s].q,           // CALL or PUT\n    double strike;      // Strike price\n    double spot;        // Spot price\n    double q;           // Dividend yield\n    double r;           // Risk-free rate\n    double t;           // Time to maturity\n    double vol;         // Volatility\n    double value;       // Expected value (for validation)\n    double tol;         // Tolerance\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays view of the option set, used for coalesced device access.\n// The pricing inputs live in pinned host memory so that host/device transfers can\n// overlap with kernel execution.\nstruct OptionSoA {\n    double* strike = nullptr;\n    double* spot = nullptr;\n    double* q = nullptr;\n    double* r = nullptr;\n    double* t = nullptr;\n    double* vol = nullptr;\n    uint8_t* type = nullptr;\n};\n\n// Standard normal cumulative distribution function\ninline double cumulativeNormal(const double x) noexcept {\n    return 0.5 * (1.0 + std::erf(x * M_SQRT1_2));\n}\n\n// Standard normal probability density function\ninline double normalPDF(const double x) noexcept {\n    return M_1_SQRTPI * M_SQRT1_2 * exp(-0.5 * x * x);\n}\n\n// Black-Scholes formula for European options, one option per thread.\n//\n// Both the call and the put expression are evaluated unconditionally and selected at\n// the end: within a warp the option types are mixed, so a branch would serialize the\n// two paths anyway. N(-d) is formed as 0.5 * (1 - erf(d / sqrt(2))), which is the same\n// sequence of floating-point operations the scalar code performs for\n// cumulativeNormal(-d) because erf is exactly odd.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ strikeArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ spotArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ qArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ rArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ tArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ volArr,\n                                                          const uint8_t* __restrict__ typeArr,\n                                                          double* __restrict__ prices,\n                                                          const size_t n) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double S = spotArr[i];\n    const double K = strikeArr[i];\n    const double r = rArr[i];\n    const double q = qArr[i];\n    const double T = tArr[i];\n    const double sigma = volArr[i];\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        prices[i] = 0.0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double e1 = erf(d1 * M_SQRT1_2);\n    const double e2 = erf(d2 * M_SQRT1_2);\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double spotDiscount = S * exp(-q * T);\n\n    const double call = spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e1)) - K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e2));\n    const double put = K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e2)) - spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e1));\n\n    prices[i] = (typeArr[i] == CALL) ? call : put;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, 100.00, 110.00, 0.10, 0.10, 0.10, 0.15, 0.0408, 1.0e-3},\n    }};\n}\n\n// Generate a larger set of options by scaling the test set\nvoid generateOptions(std::vector<OptionInput>& options, OptionSoA& soa, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n\n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n\n        soa.strike[i] = options[i].strike;\n        soa.spot[i] = options[i].spot;\n        soa.q[i] = options[i].q;\n        soa.r[i] = options[i].r;\n        soa.t[i] = options[i].t;\n        soa.vol[i] = options[i].vol;\n        soa.type[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(options[i].type);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) {\n        const double computed = results[i];\n        const double expected = options[i].value;\n        const double error = fabs(computed - expected);\n        const double relError = error / (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = 10000;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numOptions = atoll(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&soa.type, numOptions * sizeof(uint8_t), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaHostAlloc(&h_prices, numOptions * sizeof(double), cudaHostAllocDefault));\n\n    // Generate options\n    std::vector<OptionInput> options;\n    generateOptions(options, soa, numOptions);\n\n    // The work is split into chunks that are streamed N(-d) is formed as 0.5 * (1 - erf(d / sqrt(2))), which is the same\n// sequence of floating-point operations the scalar code performs for\n// cumulativeNormal(-d) because erf is exactly odd.\n__global__ __launch_bounds__(256) void blackScholesKernel(const double* __restrict__ strikeArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ spotArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ qArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ rArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ tArr,\n                                                          const double* __restrict__ volArr,\n                                                          const uint8_t* __restrict__ typeArr,\n                                                          double* __restrict__ prices,\n                                                          const size_t n) {\n    const size_t i = static_cast<size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) {\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double S = spotArr[i];\n    const double K = strikeArr[i];\n    const double r = rArr[i];\n    const double q = qArr[i];\n    const double T = tArr[i];\n    const double sigma = volArr[i];\n\n    if (T <= 0.0 || sigma <= 0.0) {\n        prices[i] = 0.0;\n        return;\n    }\n\n    const double d1 = (log(S / K) + (r - q + 0.5 * sigma * sigma) * T) / (sigma * sqrt(T));\n    const double d2 = d1 - sigma * sqrt(T);\n\n    const double e1 = erf(d1 * M_SQRT1_2);\n    const double e2 = erf(d2 * M_SQRT1_2);\n\n    const double discount = exp(-r * T);\n    const double spotDiscount = S * exp(-q * T);\n\n    const double call = spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e1)) - K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 + e2));\n    const double put = K * discount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e2)) - spotDiscount * (0.5 * (1.0 - e1));\n\n    prices[i] = (typeArr[i] == CALL) ? call : put;\n}\n\n// Standard test cases for validation\ninline constexpr std::array<OptionInput, 7> getTestOptions() noexcept {\n    return {{\n        {CALL, 40.00, 42.00, 0.04, 0.08, 0.75, soa, const size_t numOptions) {\n    constexpr auto testOptions = getTestOptions();\n    options.resize(numOptions);\n\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < numOptions; ++i) {\n        // Cycle through test options and vary parameters slightly\n        const OptionInput& base = testOptions[i % testOptions.size()];\n        options[i] = base;\n\n        // Add some variation for larger datasets\n        const double factor = 1.0 + 0.1 * (i / static_cast<double>(testOptions.size()));\n        options[i].spot *= factor;\n        options[i].strike *= factor;\n\n        soa.strike[i] = options[i].strike;\n        soa.spot[i] = options[i].spot;\n        soa.q[i] = options[i].q;\n        soa.r[i] = options[i].r;\n        soa.t[i] = options[i].t;\n        soa.vol[i] = options[i].vol;\n        soa.type[i] = static_cast<uint8_t>(options[i].type);\n    }\n}\n\nbool validateResults(const std::vector<OptionInput>& options,\n                     const std::vector<double>& results) {\n    bool allPassed = true;\n    const int numChecks = std::min(static_cast<size_t>(10), options.size());\n\n    printf(\"Checking computed option prices:\\n\");\n    for (int i = 0; i < numChecks; ++i) (fabs(expected) + 1e-10);\n\n        printf(\"  Option %d: computed=%.4f, expected=%.4f, rel_error=%.4e\\n\",\n               i, computed, expected, relError);\n\n        // Relaxed validation - just check values are positive and reasonable\n        if (computed < 0.0 || computed > 1000.0 || std::isnan(computed) || std::isinf(computed)) {\n            printf(\"Validation failed at option %d: invalid value %.4f\\n\", i, computed);\n            allPassed = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    return allPassed;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of options to price (default: 10000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation against known values\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    size_t numOptions = numOptions);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n    // Nothing to transfer or launch; the streaming path below assumes a non-empty set.\n    if (numOptions Summary of the CUDA parallelization:\n\n**`black-scholes/black_scholes.cpp`**\n- `blackScholesKernel`: one thread per option, `__launch_bounds__(256)`, reading a **structure-of-arrays** layout so every load is fully coalesced (the original AoS `OptionInput` is 72 B/element with fields the kernel never touches).\n- Branch-free call/put selection: both prices are computed and selected with a ternary. Option types alternate with period 7, so nearly every warp is mixed and a real branch would serialize both paths anyway. `N(-d)` is formed as `0.5*(1 - erf(d/√2))`, which is the identical operation sequence the scalar `cumulativeNormal(-d)` performs (erf is exactly odd), so no extra accuracy is given up.\n- **Streamed pipeline**: work is split into 4 MB-option chunks cycled over 4 streams, so H2D transfer of one chunk overlaps the kernel and D2H of its predecessors (the RTX 3090's two copy engines give full duplex). Host input/output buffers are pinned (`cudaHostAlloc`) for DMA.\n- Timed region covers H2D + kernel + D2H — the full path from host inputs to host results. Context creation and a no-op warm-up launch (module load) are done before the timer; without the warm-up the first-launch module load alone cost ~55 ms.\n- Option generation now also fills the pinned SoA buffers; the AoS vector is still built because validation reads `options[i].value`. Argument parsing, printing, and validation are unchanged. `-n 0` is short-circuited (the streaming path assumes non-empty) and still prints the same lines as before, `-nan` included.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`**: enables the CUDA language, compiles `black_scholes.cpp` as CUDA via `set_source_files_properties` (executable name and file names unchanged), `CMAKE_CUDA_ARCHITECTURES native`, and `--prec-div=true --prec-sqrt=true --fmad=false` so device arithmetic stays IEEE-faithful. No new dependencies.\n\n**Correctness**: compared against a rebuild of the original serial code at n = 1, 7, 1000, 999983, 5M, 10M — all sums, min/max and samples agree to ~1e-15 relative (worst observed deviation 3e-13 on a near-cancelling put). The FNV `Hash:` line differs, which is unavoidable: it hashes raw doubles, and CUDA's `erf`/`log`/`exp` differ from glibc's by up to ~1 ulp. `-v` validation passes at every size.\n\n**Throughput** (RTX 3090, PCIe x8):\n\n| n | serial | CUDA | speedup |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| 10k | 10.9 M opt/s | 88 M opt/s | 8x |\n| 1M | 11.1 M opt/s | 192 M opt/s | 17x |\n| 50M | 10.9 M opt/s | 249 M opt/s | 23x |\n| 100M | — | 255 M opt/s | 23x |\n\nAt large n this is PCIe-bandwidth-bound, not compute-bound: 49 B of input per option is ~12 GB/s of H2D traffic at the observed rate, which saturates this link. The kernel itself is far faster than that; going beyond this ceiling would require generating the option inputs on the device instead of transferring them, which I did not do since it would specialize the program to its synthetic input generator rather than pricing an array that exists in host memory."}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2509,"cache_read_input_tokens":52623,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2509},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"73e263f0-d975-45c5-890a-5d819de7b75e","uuid":"9ef76daf-cb22-4b5c-8d2c-b76a742b4abd","timestamp":"2026-09-03T20:08:00.162Z","request_id":"req_011Ceh7KyXECyPWZjdjgynDt"}
